### Q: How is product quality ensured? | company

A: GIBO's enterprise standards are established based on industry standards and relevant standards from European and American countries, designed so GIBO products can meet the needs of most global markets. Most project standards exceed national standards. We have strict quality control procedures and methods to ensure factory-delivered products meet market and customer demands.

From a design perspective:
Strict design review, standardized design DFMEA, eliminating design hazards;
High-standard material selection, ensuring core components meet safety, health, environmental, and durability standards;
Strictly controlled design change management procedures; after product finalization, processes and materials cannot be arbitrarily changed unless necessary.

From a production perspective:
Complete quality process: IQC-PQC-OQC, each step fully traceable, ensuring materials meet design requirements;
Complete standardized operation process assurance: From new product trial production, standardized documents such as product specification sheets, PPAP, operation instructions, and critical inspection dimensions are output;
Every new product undergoes a trial production process to reduce product risks while training new product workers' operational proficiency;
Key core components such as electronic modules, solenoid valves, and power supplies all undergo 100% full inspection before assembly production;
The R&D laboratory has an annual type testing plan for every product category or core component.

---

Original factory technical personnel will provide remote guidance for troubleshooting or parameter adjustment, ensuring customized functions operate normally.

Original factory accessories have been jointly tuned, with guaranteed compatibility and stability.

### Q: Water inside the sensor nozzle? | trouble

A: Sensor nozzles must undergo high-pressure 0.9Mpa, low-pressure 0.05Mpa, and 30-second pressure-holding water testing before shipment. After air-blowing to remove residual water, some sensor nozzles may still have a small amount of residual water, which is not a quality issue and can be used with confidence.
Sensor nozzle material:
Exterior: ABS engineering plastic material with crystal diamond chrome plating, scratch-resistant, lasting like new.
Interior: Valve body uses environmentally friendly POM all-plastic material, no heavy metal leaching, compliant with drinking water standards.
Rubber diaphragm: EPDM material, non-toxic and safe, compliant with NSF certification.
Note: When cleaning the sensor nozzle, use a dry, soft cloth to wipe clean. Do not rinse with water or use rough linen, iron sand, or steel wool to prevent scratching the sensor nozzle and sensor window, which could affect sensor lifespan and performance.
Water savings:
Integrated flow-limiting design with Swiss Neoperl aerator, flow rate 0.07-0.08L/s, compliant with national water efficiency Level 1 standard (0.1 L/s), saving over 30% water compared to ordinary faucets.
Note:
GIBO Xiaoxibao sensor nozzle (0.07L/s), Level 1 water efficiency (0.1L/s), Level 2 water efficiency (0.125L/s), Level 3 water efficiency.

---

### Q: Will the circuit and battery be affected by moisture? | trouble

A: (1) The circuit is potted with epoxy resin for good waterproof and moisture-proof capability.
(2) The battery packaging has a certain protective capability.
(3) The sensor nozzle upper body water inlet end has an anti-overflow rubber gasket.
(4) The Micro USB charging port is potted and has a waterproof rubber protective plug. After charging, ensure the waterproof rubber protective plug is in a sealed state to ensure effective waterproofing.
Water temperature issue:
The sensor nozzle material itself can withstand -20℃~120℃, but it is recommended that the water supply temperature be between 1℃-45℃, with a maximum temperature recommended not to exceed 75℃, mainly to prevent high-temperature skin burns.

---

### Q: Could it cause cold water backflow, making hot water not hot? | trouble

A: The cold-hot water mixing issue does not depend on the GIBO Xiaoxibao sensor nozzle but is determined by the faucet. If cold-hot water backflow issues exist, it is recommended to purchase a check valve separately and install it at the water inlet.
Water pressure issue:
Applicable water pressure: The suitable water pressure range for the GIBO Xiaoxibao sensor nozzle is 0.05-0.8MPa. Within this pressure range, normal use is possible (normal municipal water supply pressure is around 0.2MPa).
Faucet pressure capacity: Some faucet outlet curved pipes on the market are not designed for pressure holding. After installing the GIBO Xiaoxibao sensor nozzle, the outlet curved pipe becomes pressure-bearing as water is opened/closed through the sensor nozzle. When inlet water pressure exceeds 0.2MPa, faucet replacement is recommended.
Suggestion:
Before purchasing the GIBO Xiaoxibao sensor nozzle, check the tap water pressure in your residential area. Normal pressure is around 0.2MPa. If water pressure exceeds 0.2MPa, it is recommended to change to a faucet with a pressure-bearing curved pipe design.
During infrequent water usage periods or when the faucet is not used for extended periods, it is recommended to turn off the faucet handle.
Battery and charging issues:
Due to lithium battery characteristics, it is recommended to fully charge new sensor nozzles or those unused for over a year before use.
The sensor nozzle uses a universal Micro USB interface for charging. A power bank connected with a data cable can charge the sensor nozzle (turn off the faucet handle during charging). The sensor nozzle can also be unscrewed for charging (USB charging cable and charger are self-provided).

---

### Q: After closing the water, there is still a small amount of flow. Is this normal? | trouble

A: After the hand leaves the sensing range, dripping or small water flow is abnormal and indicates a problem. There are 2 main causes:
(1) Water pressure is too low; installing a booster pump is recommended.
(2) Small impurities such as thread seal tape are blocking the diaphragm. Disassembly and cleaning will resolve the issue.

---

### Q: Water output is low, how to resolve? | trouble

A: First check 2 core points: (1) Whether water pressure meets the standard; if not, install a booster pump. (2) Whether the filter screen is clogged; after rinsing clean, the water output will return to normal.

---

### Q: Batteries run out quickly. Is this a product quality issue? | trouble

A: Most likely, the wrong batteries are being used! Do not use low-quality carbon batteries. Switch to branded alkaline batteries such as Nanfu or Duracell.
Product battery usage time reference:
Non-digital-display sensor faucets: 4 AA alkaline batteries last over 1 year;
Digital-display sensor faucets: 4 AA alkaline batteries last over 6 months;
Soap-faucet combo sensor faucets: 4 AA alkaline batteries last over 3 months. Using a power adapter for power supply is recommended.

---

### Q: The temperature digital display is not lit. How to handle? | trouble

A: It's basically a battery issue: (1) First check if the battery installation direction is correct. (2) If the direction is correct, replace with 4 new AA alkaline batteries, and the digital display will recover.

---

### Q: If home water pressure is relatively low, will using this product affect constant temperature performance and water output? | trouble

A: It will not affect performance. This product's operating water pressure range is 0.05MPa~0.6MPa, covering most household and hotel normal water pressures. Even if water pressure fluctuates slightly, the HW05 thermostatic valve core will automatically adjust to ensure constant temperature performance (maximum deviation ≤3℃), with smooth water output and no stuttering.

---

### Q: The panel touch is not sensitive. Will water stains affect it? | trouble

A: The panel supports waterproof and anti-false-touch design. Small amounts of water stains will not affect operation. If touch is not sensitive, wipe the panel with a dry cloth. Avoid touching the panel with sharp objects to prevent damage.

---

### Q: The hydropower generator is not working. Will it affect usage? | trouble

A: No. The hydropower generator is mainly used to charge the lithium battery. Even if the generator fails, the lithium battery can provide normal backup power supply and will not affect the use of core functions. If the generator fails, the buzzer will emit cyclic reminders. Contact after-sales for inspection and repair.

---

### Q: During use, the buzzer keeps sounding. What is the reason? | trouble

A: The buzzer sounding is a fault or prompt signal. Cross-check as follows: (1) 1 beep: Initialization complete, pre-drainage start, pairing successful (normal prompt). (2) 3 beeps: Pre-drainage stop (normal prompt). (3) Intermittent beeping: Low battery warning, NTC abnormality, generator fault (corresponding to digital display fault indicators; handle according to prompts or contact after-sales).

---

### Q: If power suddenly cuts off during use, will there be water leakage? | trouble

A: No. This product has power failure protection. When power is interrupted, all solenoid valves will automatically close, stopping water output. If power is cut while the unit is off, the solenoid valves will remain closed, completely eliminating leakage risks.

---

### Q: What causes insensitive sensing? | trouble

A: Insensitive sensing generally refers to sensing response time. Most sensors on the market have a set response time of 512ms. During faucet product use, some users, based on their experience, feel the water-on time is too slow or water flow is intermittent, reporting it as insensitive sensing. The main reason is that software optimization is insufficient and does not consider the user experience.
GIBO's proprietary agile sensing technology can achieve water on/off as fast as 64ms, 5-10 times faster than ordinary sensors, with fast response speed and excellent user experience.

---

### Q: Sensing is normal but the valve does not open. What is the problem? | trouble

A: Generally, the following causes exist:
The solenoid valve connection wire is not properly connected;
The customer's self-equipped solenoid valve's switching pulse does not match the module output signal;
The module output current is insufficient;
The solenoid valve iron core is stuck or not properly assembled.

---

### Q: The indicator light is normal but no liquid comes out. Why? | trouble

A: A. There is water on the power button. Wipe it off for normal switching and operation.
B: Check if the soap liquid is depleted.
C: Check if the liquid bottle is not tightly screwed on or if the liquid tube has come loose.

---

### Q: When reaching out to trigger sensing, the indicator light does not illuminate. Why? | trouble

A: Use the elimination method to resolve.
Confirm whether there are foreign objects, foam, water droplets, or mist blocking the sensor window. Clean it.
Confirm whether the unit is powered on (press and hold for 2 seconds; if the white indicator stays lit for 2 seconds, the unit is powered on).
Refer to points 1.1/1.2 to confirm whether the battery is fully charged.
After powering on a new unit and dispensing liquid once, subsequent sensing triggers no response.
Confirm whether the hand wash station is placed in a location with strong direct light shining on the sensor window. If so, change the location.
Confirm whether the hand wash station is placed on highly reflective surfaces such as stainless steel, mirror surfaces, or white glossy surfaces. If so, change the location. Alternatively, place a dark towel or coaster under the hand wash station to resolve the reflection issue.

---

### Q: After installing batteries, the light on the power button stays on but sensing has no response? | trouble

A: There is water on the power button. Wipe it off for normal switching and operation.

---

### Q: After refilling with self-prepared liquid, the indicator light is on and the pump working sound can be heard, but no liquid comes out? | trouble

A: 1. Your prepared liquid may be too thick. It needs to be diluted by adding 4 times the soap liquid volume in water.
2. Does the liquid you prepared contain particles? This product does not support liquids containing particles because the pump nozzle is very fine, and particles will clog the pump nozzle, causing inability to dispense liquid.

---

### Q: After refilling with self-prepared liquid, the operation indicator light does not illuminate during sensing, and liquid does not come out? | trouble

A: You probably moved the battery box cover when reassembling the unit. Re-tighten the battery box cover once, then power on again.

---

### Q: What causes poor flushing force? | trouble

A: 1. Check if the toilet drain outlet is correctly installed.
2. Check if there is a return bend in the drain pipe.
3. If there is a return bend, a 20mm small opening needs to be cut at the drain outlet and flange ring to form air pressure relief.

---

### Q: Sensor sanitary ware frequently has false triggers and random water discharge. How to handle? | trouble

A: False triggering and self-discharging of sensor sanitary ware are mostly caused by environmental light, mirror reflections, and electromagnetic signal interference. GIBO's full product line is equipped with dTOF Laser sensing technology, combined with 23 types of light source interference suppression algorithms and 30 types of electromagnetic anti-interference enhancement algorithms, able to avoid such failures from the underlying level. In daily use, promptly clean sensor window stains, keep away from high-power electrical appliances, further reduce interference impact, and ensure stable equipment operation.

---

### Q: After powering on, the sensor faucet cannot dispense water. What are the troubleshooting steps? | trouble

A: If a sensor faucet cannot dispense water after power-on, troubleshoot step by step from simple to complex. First, check if the power supply battery or external power source is normal. Then check if the sensor window is blocked by dust or moisture. Next, check if the pipeline is clogged. Finally, confirm the solenoid valve's working status. GIBO sensor faucets have a simple structural design, core components are 100% fully tested, with few failure points. Regular maintenance personnel can quickly complete repairs.

---

### Q: The sensor module indicator light keeps flashing. How to resolve? | trouble

A: Abnormal flashing of the sensor module indicator light is generally caused by unstable power supply voltage or poor line connection. GIBO's self-developed sensor modules have built-in voltage stabilization protection circuits compatible with multiple voltage levels. After this fault occurs, prioritize checking the power supply circuit and whether terminal connections are loose. Re-tighten the circuit and check the voltage. Once voltage stabilizes, the indicator light will return to normal status.

---

### Q: After installing the sensor nozzle, the equipment does not work. What is the problem? | trouble

A: After installing the GIBO sensor nozzle, if it cannot work normally, first check if the installation position is loose and if sealing is adequate. Then check if the sensor surface is completely blocked by water stains or debris. This product is a retrofit-free lightweight accessory with a simple structure. Most faults are caused by installation issues and external obstructions. Re-secure and clean the sensor area to restore use.

---

### Q: Smart shower sensor failure. What are the routine troubleshooting methods? | trouble

A: For smart shower sensor failure, prioritize cleaning the sensor window of attached moisture, scale, and dust. The high humidity environment of bathrooms easily blocks sensor signals. GIBO smart showers are equipped with multiple anti-interference algorithms and have extremely low inherent failure rates. If cleaning does not resolve the issue, further check the power supply lines and voltage to locate the fault point step by step.

---

### Q: Smart toilet sensor lid opening fails. How should it be inspected and repaired? | trouble

A: For smart toilet sensor lid opening failure, first thoroughly clean the sensor area. Moisture and stains are the main causes of sensor signal blockage. Then check if the entire unit's power supply is normal and if wiring has aged or loosened. GIBO smart toilet sensor components have undergone rigorous testing. Performance is stable under normal use. Basic troubleshooting can resolve most lid opening failures.

---

### Q: Sensor sanitary ware cannot be used after water immersion. What is the emergency treatment method? | trouble

A: If sensor sanitary ware is accidentally submerged, immediately cut off the power supply to prevent circuit short circuits from burning out components. Place the equipment in a ventilated, dry area to air dry naturally. Only test by powering on after it is completely dry. GIBO's full product line uses multi-layer waterproof processing. After minor immersion and drying, most units can recover. If water immersion was severe, contact after-sales for internal circuit inspection.

---

### Q: Commercial sensor sanitary ware cannot start in low-temperature winter environments? | trouble

A: GIBO's full line of sensor sanitary ware has been designed for low-temperature tolerance. Normal indoor low temperatures allow normal operation. If equipment cannot start in winter, prioritize checking the power supply battery activity. Low temperatures significantly reduce battery discharge capacity. Replace with batteries suitable for low temperatures. For outdoor equipment, also check if pipelines are frozen and blocked.

---

### Q: Water leakage at sensor sanitary ware joint positions. How to handle? | trouble

A: Pipeline and joint leakage in sensor sanitary ware is mostly caused by aging seals or insufficient thread tightening. First close the water source, re-tighten the joint threads. If leakage persists, directly replace with new seals. GIBO factory-standard configuration includes high-quality sealing accessories. Regular seal replacement can effectively prevent leakage failures.

---

### Q: Sensor sanitary ware sensing distance suddenly becomes shorter. What is the problem? | trouble

A: Shortened sensing distance is primarily caused by accumulated stains and mist on the sensor window, weakening the sensor signal. GIBO dTOF Laser sensor module has stable sensing distance. Daily regular wiping of the sensor surface to keep it clean and clear can maintain normal sensing range without needing to adjust equipment parameters.

---

### Q: Multiple sensor devices installed side by side causing mutual interference. How to resolve? | trouble

A: Mutual interference when multiple sensor sanitary ware devices are centrally installed is a common issue in commercial public areas. GIBO products are equipped with 30 types of electromagnetic anti-interference enhancement algorithms, with anti-interference reaching military 4A level. If interference occurs, appropriately increase the installation spacing between devices while keeping away from high-power electrical appliances, providing dual protection against interference effects.

---

### Q: Large batch products experiencing similar quality issues. What is the after-sales handling process? | trouble

A: If multiple devices from the same batch exhibit identical faults, and after on-site verification by both parties it is confirmed to be a product issue, follow the official after-sales rules. GIBO will complete replacement, replenishment, or refund within 7 working days and fully bear round-trip logistics shipping costs, minimizing losses for engineering projects and purchasing parties to the greatest extent.

---

### Q: Sensor sanitary ware damaged by lightning or unstable voltage. Is it covered under warranty? | trouble

A: Equipment damage caused by lightning strikes, utility power instantaneous high voltage, or external voltage abnormal fluctuations belongs to external force and power supply environment issues and is not covered within the 1-year free repair warranty scope. GIBO can provide paid repair and replacement parts services. It is simultaneously recommended to install voltage stabilization and lightning protection equipment in areas with complex power supply conditions for protection.

---

### Q: Sensor faucet valve body sticking, frequent switch failure? | trouble

A: Sensor faucet solenoid valve sticking is generally caused by pipeline sediment and impurities entering the valve body interior. Disassemble the valve body after closing the water source, thoroughly clean internal dirt, and reassemble to restore function. GIBO core solenoid valves undergo full factory inspection, with pre-filter design, significantly reducing sticking failures.

---

### Q: Sensor soap dispenser pump body makes abnormal noise, liquid output is unstable? | trouble

A: Abnormal sound from the soap dispenser pump body and inconsistent liquid output are mostly caused by pump wear or air in the pipeline. First check the liquid storage bottle seal, then lubricate and maintain the pump body. Replace original factory pump body accessories when wear is severe. GIBO pump bodies have high durability, with abnormal noise failures being relatively rare under normal use.

---

### Q: Smart toilet flushing force decreases. How to handle? | trouble

A: Decreased smart toilet flushing force is mainly caused by clogged water inlet filter mesh, waterway scale buildup, and insufficient water pressure. Regularly disassemble and clean the water inlet filter mesh and pipeline scale. Simultaneously confirm that the incoming water pressure is within the equipment's suitable range to restore normal flushing performance.

---

### Q: Sensor hand dryer completely fails to start. Step-by-step troubleshooting? | trouble

A: If the sensor hand dryer unit does not work, troubleshoot in the order of: power supply → sensor area → motor. First, check the external power supply or battery. Second, clean the sensor window. Finally, check the motor wiring. GIBO hand dryer circuits have neat layouts with clear fault points, facilitating maintenance.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment screen is black but can still work normally? | trouble

A: If the equipment functions normally with only the display screen black, this is a separate display component failure. The main circuit and sensor module have no issues. No need to disassemble the entire unit. Contact after-sales to replace the same model display panel. This does not affect the equipment's core usage functions.

---

### Q: Sensor module overheats severely. Is there a safety hazard? | trouble

A: Mild heating is a normal circuit phenomenon. If the module becomes abnormally hot to the touch, immediately cut power and discontinue use. This is most likely caused by abnormal circuit load or component aging. GIBO sensor modules have overheat protection. When high-temperature phenomena occur, promptly send for inspection to avoid safety accidents.

---

### Q: Sensor flush valve winter pipeline freeze-blockage. What is the thawing method? | trouble

A: For northern winter flush valve pipeline freeze-blockage, never use open flame for heating. Use warm water to slowly melt the ice blockage. After thawing, drain residual water from the pipeline and install insulation cotton or anti-freeze accessories. GIBO has specialized anti-freeze model flush valves. Prioritize selection for northern outdoor projects.

---

### Q: Sensor soap dispenser filled with wrong soap liquid causing pipeline blockage? | trouble

A: Using soap liquid with excessively high viscosity will cause pipeline and pump body blockage. Completely drain all existing soap liquid. Repeatedly flush the entire pipeline and pump body with clean water. Then replace with standard hand soap or foam liquid compatible with the equipment to prevent re-blockage.

---

### Q: Sensor faucet water outlet direction is misaligned. How to adjust? | trouble

A: If the water outlet nozzle is misaligned, directly manually micro-adjust the angle. If the base is loose, first close the water source, tighten the fixing screws, then calibrate the water outlet direction. GIBO sensor faucet base structures are stable. Displacement issues are rare under normal use.

---

### Q: Smart shower remote control fails. Pairing fails? | trouble

A: For wireless remote control failure, first replace the remote control batteries, then re-pair the remote control with the main unit. If connection still cannot be established, check whether the main unit's wireless module is blocked by moisture. Clean it and try again.

---

### Q: Sensor hand dryer makes abnormal noise during operation. What is the problem? | trouble

A: Abnormal noise during hand dryer operation is mostly caused by fan blades touching debris or bearing wear. Cut power, open the air duct, and clear debris. If blades are fine, it is bearing aging. Replace with original factory bearing accessories to eliminate the noise.

---

### Q: Customized sensor equipment parameters are chaotic. How to restore? | trouble

A: For customized equipment with parameter chaos, do not rewrite the program on your own. Contact GIBO technical personnel to obtain standard parameters or factory reset operation methods. Remote assistance will be provided for restoration, ensuring customized functions operate normally.

---

### Q: Sensor nozzle shell cracked. Risk of water leakage and electric shock? | trouble

A: After the shell cracks, waterproof capability is reduced, and water ingress can easily cause electric leakage. Small cracks can be repaired with specialized waterproof adhesive. Severe cracks require direct replacement with the same model shell. After repair, be sure to test waterproofing and circuit safety before returning to use.

---

### Q: Sensor module signal is intermittent. Poor contact? | trouble

A: For unstable module signals, focus on checking ribbon cables and terminal connections. Long-term vibration and temperature differences can cause terminals to loosen. Re-plug and tighten each circuit one by one to ensure firm connections. The signal will recover stability.

---

### Q: Sensor faucet water pressure too high triggering non-stop water flow? | trouble

A: Excessive incoming water pressure can prevent the valve body from resetting normally, causing continuous flow. Install a pressure reducing valve at the water inlet to adjust water pressure to the equipment's standard range. This will resolve continuous flow failures caused by excessive water pressure.

---

### Q: Sensor hand dryer air outlet has excessive dust accumulation. How to clean? | trouble

A: First cut power. Use a brush and vacuum cleaner to clean the air outlet and internal air duct dust. Regular cleaning ensures proper air volume and prevents dust accumulation from affecting motor heat dissipation, extending equipment service life.

---

### Q: Smart toilet lid is loose and makes noise. How to tighten? | trouble

A: After extended use, toilet lid screws may loosen, causing noise during opening and closing. Locate the fixing screws at the bottom of the lid and tighten them one by one. If hinges are severely worn, directly replace with original factory hinge accessories.

---

### Q: Outdoor sensor equipment fails after direct sunlight exposure? | trouble

A: Strong direct light can interfere with ordinary sensor equipment. GIBO is equipped with 23 types of light source suppression algorithms, with stronger resistance to bright light. If failure still occurs, install a sunshade to avoid direct sunlight on the sensor area.

---

### Q: Sensor soap dispenser outputs liquid sometimes fast, sometimes slow. Air in the pipeline? | trouble

A: Uneven liquid output speed is basically caused by air entering the liquid storage bottle or pipeline. Check if the liquid storage bottle seal cap is tightly screwed on. Inspect pipeline gaps. After sealing air entry points, liquid output speed will recover to uniformity.

---

### Q: Multiple modules connected in series causing total system failure? | trouble

A: If series-connected modules as a whole cannot work, troubleshoot segment by segment starting from the main power supply. Find the faulty single unit, disconnect the series circuit, repair the faulty module individually. The remaining equipment can temporarily resume use.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment cannot start after long-term shutdown? | trouble

A: After long-term equipment idling, battery depletion and wire oxidation are the main causes. First, replace with new batteries entirely. Then polish oxidized terminal connections. Power on and test. Basic maintenance can restart the equipment.

---

### Q: Sensor flush valve spray head scale blockage causing reduced water output? | trouble

A: Long-term use accumulates scale on the spray head, causing reduced water output. Remove the spray head, soak in descaling agent to clean scale. Rinse clean and reinstall. Water output will recover to original state. Regular descaling maintenance is recommended.

---

### Q: Smart shower thermostatic function fails. Water temperature is uncontrolled? | trouble

A: Thermostatic module failure can cause water temperature to fluctuate between hot and cold, even losing control. Immediately stop using the equipment, close the water source, and contact GIBO after-sales for inspection or thermostatic component replacement. Strictly prohibit continued use with faults to prevent scalding risks.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment buttons stick and cannot be operated? | trouble

A: Physical button sticking is mostly caused by dust and moisture entering button gaps. Cut power, remove the button panel, clean internal dirt, check button spring contacts. After repair, normal press operation can resume.

---

### Q: No response to sensing. What to do? | trouble

A: First do 2 simple steps: (1) Replace with new batteries. For AC220V models, confirm power is connected. (2) Check if the sensor window is clean and unobstructed. If still unresponsive, contact after-sales to adjust sensing distance or inspect and repair.

---

### Q: Digital display screen not lit/display inaccurate. What to do? | trouble

A: A1: (1) Not lit: Most likely no water output (digital display is linked to water output; it lights up automatically with water flow) or low battery (the digital display will simultaneously show a low battery indicator, and the buzzer will alert). You can wake it up via touch panel or charge the lithium battery. (2) Inaccurate display: The NTC temperature sensor position may have shifted. Adjust the sensor to the water flow contact point. This product's sensor accuracy is ±2℃, which is within the normal range.

---

### Q: Wireless remote control cannot pair. What to do? | trouble

A: A1: Please strictly follow the pairing procedure: Within 2 minutes of the receiver being powered on, press and hold the button remote for 5s, touch remote for 3-5s then short press once, infrared sensor remote continuously trigger sensing for 8s. Upon successful pairing, the buzzer will beep once and the digital display screen will flash once. If still unable to pair, restart the receiver and try again. This product can pair with any same-model receiver.

---

### Q: Digital display shows fault indicator (e.g., E1). How to handle? | trouble

A: A2: E1 indicates NTC temperature sensor abnormality (short circuit/open circuit). First check if the sensor wiring is loose. After adjusting the wiring, restart the system. If the fault indicator still appears, contact after-sales to replace the sensor. For other fault indicators, refer to the product manual or contact after-sales for consultation.

---

### Q: Suddenly no water output/cannot turn off water. What to do? | trouble

A: A3: (1) No water output: Check lithium battery level (low battery will shut down), whether the solenoid valve is normal (can restart system for self-check), and whether water pressure meets standards. (2) Cannot turn off water: Most likely solenoid valve fault or power failure protection not reset. Restart the system. If still unable to close, disconnect power and contact after-sales to inspect and repair the solenoid valve.

---

### Q: Sensor flush valve continuously flushes without stopping. What is the cause, how to repair? | trouble

A: Continuous flow and non-stop flushing of sensor flush valves are mainly caused by external interference in the sensor area or valve body sticking. GIBO sensor flush valves adopt military-grade EMC anti-interference design, significantly reducing interference-type failures. When encountering this issue, first clean the sensor area and adjust the installation angle. If unresolved, check for impurities inside the valve body. Cleaning will restore normal use.

---

### Q: Sensor soap dispenser does not dispense liquid after power-on. What are the common fault causes? | trouble

A: Sensor soap dispenser failure to dispense liquid is a high-frequency fault in commercial scenarios. It mainly falls into four categories: insufficient soap liquid volume, inlet/outlet pipeline blockage, pump body sticking, and sensor area obstruction. GIBO sensor soap dispenser pump bodies have undergone tens of thousands of durability tests with stable quality. Daily operation and maintenance simply requires regular soap liquid replenishment, pipeline clearing, and sensor window cleaning to effectively avoid such issues.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment battery consumption speed is too fast. What fault is this? | trouble

A: Rapid battery depletion is caused on one hand by abnormal circuit leakage in the equipment, and on the other hand by continuous strong interference in the surrounding area causing the equipment to trigger frequently. All GIBO sensor products use self-developed low-power consumption circuits, with excellent normal battery life performance. When troubleshooting, first clear interference sources. If battery depletion remains too fast, send the circuit module for inspection.

---

### Q: Sensor flush valve water output is intermittent. What repair methods are available? | trouble

A: Sensor flush valve output fluctuating and intermittent should be investigated from three directions: water pressure stability, internal pipeline impurity blockage, and valve body sticking. First clear the water inlet pipe and clean dirt. Then check if water pressure is within the equipment's suitable range. Finally, inspect and repair the valve body assembly. Handle item by item to restore normal water output.

---

### Q: Sensor hand dryer air output volume decreases. What are the common fault causes? | trouble

A: For reduced sensor hand dryer air output, first check if the air outlet and air inlet are blocked by hair or debris. This is the most common cause. Next, check the motor's running condition. Long-term use motor wear will also cause air volume decrease. GIBO hand dryer motors have undergone aging tests. Regular air duct cleaning can extend service life.

---

### Q: After replacing old sensor modules, incompatibility faults occur? | trouble

A: After replacing old equipment with new modules, failure to work normally is mostly caused by interface and parameter mismatches. It is recommended to directly select GIBO universal sensor modules. This module has strong interface compatibility, with parameters compatible with the vast majority of old sanitary ware on the market. After replacement, no additional debugging is needed and it can be put into use directly.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment repeatedly starts and stops after power-on. What fault is this? | trouble

A: Equipment repeatedly auto-starting and stopping is most likely due to two major issues: electromagnetic interference and loose wiring connections. First, survey surrounding electrical equipment and move away from high-power appliances. Then check power cables and terminal connections segment by segment, tightening loose circuits. GIBO's anti-interference design can effectively reduce the probability of such faults occurring.

---

### Q: Outdoor sensor equipment fails due to water ingress on rainy days. What to do? | trouble

A: If outdoor sensor equipment gets rained on and water enters, immediately cut power. Open the shell and thoroughly ventilate and air dry the internal circuit board and modules. Only power on and test after confirming no water stains remain. GIBO outdoor model sensor products have enhanced waterproof ratings, but long-term heavy rain immersion still poses risks. Installing a rain cover for protection is recommended.

---

### Q: Sensor nozzle frequently drips after installation. How to repair? | trouble

A: Continuous dripping after the sensor nozzle finishes use is caused by seal aging or valve assembly failure to reset. First, gently tap the valve assembly to help it reset. If ineffective, replace the internal seal ring. GIBO sensor nozzles are patented retrofit-free products. Seal accessories are durable. Regular maintenance can eliminate dripping.

---

### Q: Smart shower water temperature fluctuates between hot and cold. How to troubleshoot? | trouble

A: For smart shower water temperature fluctuations, prioritize checking whether cold and hot water inlet pressures are balanced. Next, check if the thermostatic component is malfunctioning. GIBO smart shower thermostatic modules undergo precise calibration. For areas with unstable water pressure, install a pressure regulator valve. For thermostatic faults, contact after-sales for component inspection and repair.

---

### Q: If one unit fails in a multi-linked sensor equipment setup, does it affect the entire system? | trouble

A: GIBO multi-linked combined sensor equipment uses independent circuit design. A single unit failure generally does not affect other equipment. During maintenance, simply cut power and repair the faulty unit individually. The remaining equipment can operate normally without affecting overall public area usage.

---

### Q: Sensor equipment has insects entering internally causing failure? | trouble

A: Products mostly use IP65 protection processes. In public areas, insects cannot easily enter the equipment interior to cause short circuits or signal abnormalities. After cutting power, open the shell to clean insects and debris. After completion, check the sealing strip. If gaps are too large, install insect-proof netting and perform daily protection.

---

### Q: Commercial sensor equipment failure caused by large day-night temperature differences? | trouble

A: Suitable environmental temperature is 0-65℃. Large day-night temperature differences easily produce condensation water that erodes circuits. Most products have IP65 protection processes applied before shipment. Simultaneously ensure good shell sealing to reduce condensation formation and protect the circuit board.

### Q: Between GIBO sensor faucets and Moen sensor faucets, which offers better value for money? | comparison,tap

A: GIBO and Moen sensor faucets have different positioning. Moen, as an international comprehensive sanitary ware brand, mostly uses externally sourced modules for assembled sensor faucets, with relatively high brand premium. GIBO has focused on sensor sanitary ware for 20 years, with self-developed and self-produced sensor technology, 200+ patents. If it's project bulk procurement (50+ units), GIBO's ODM customization advantage is greater—appearance, function, and packaging can all be customized as needed. If considering only brand recognition, Moen has advantages in the retail end. If considering cost-effectiveness, technical depth, and customization flexibility, GIBO is clearly superior. GIBO is also one of Moen's ODM manufacturing partners, with quality already verified by top-tier brands.

---

### Q: What are the core differences between GIBO sensor faucets and TOTO sensor faucets? | comparison,tap

A: TOTO and GIBO sensor faucets have different positioning and tracks. TOTO is a Japanese comprehensive sanitary ware giant, with sensor faucets mainly using ceramic bodies with externally sourced sensor modules. Brand influence is strong but prices are relatively high (RMB 2,000-5,000 per unit). GIBO focuses on the commercial sensor faucet field, with 200+ self-developed patents. The entire product line uses self-developed sensor motherboards and solenoid valves, with repair rates below 5‰ and more affordable prices (commercial models RMB 800-2,000). TOTO has brand advantages in high-end five-star hotels. GIBO is more competitive in chain hotels, hospitals, schools, and other engineering markets as well as ODM manufacturing. Additionally, GIBO supports full-process ODM customization; TOTO does not.

---

### Q: Between all-copper sensor faucets and zinc alloy sensor faucets, which is better? | comparison,tap

A: All-copper and zinc alloy are the two mainstream materials for sensor faucets, each with pros and cons: All-copper (H59/H62 copper casting) advantages: strong corrosion resistance, substantial texture, suitable for high-end venues. Normal use lasts 10+ years without rusting. Disadvantages: higher price, heavier weight. Zinc alloy advantages: high precision in die-casting molding, good surface treatment results, cost 30%-40% lower than all-copper. Disadvantages: long-term use may develop intergranular corrosion (commonly known as "bubbling"). Purchasing advice: For high-end hotels, hospitals, food factories, prefer all-copper models. For schools, office buildings, budget hotels, and other budget-constrained scenarios, zinc alloy models are adequate and offer better cost-effectiveness. GIBO's all-copper faucets use H59 copper gravity casting. Zinc alloy models undergo 72-hour salt spray testing. Both materials enjoy the same warranty service.

---

### Q: Between infrared sensing and microwave sensing technologies, which is more stable? | comparison,sensor

A: Infrared sensing and microwave sensing each have applicable scenarios. One cannot simply say which is more stable. Infrared sensing (passive): triggers by detecting changes in human infrared heat sources. Extremely low power consumption (standby ≤30μA), suitable for indoor bathrooms and other temperature-stable environments. Infrared advantages: low cost, low power consumption, mature application. Disadvantages: sensitivity decreases when ambient temperature approaches body temperature (e.g., high-temperature summer bathrooms). Microwave sensing (dTOF Laser/radar): detects object movement by emitting electromagnetic waves. Not affected by temperature. High sensitivity, strong anti-interference capability, but slightly higher power consumption and higher cost. GIBO matches technology to scenarios: infrared sensing for standard indoor faucets; dTOF Laser sensing for outdoor, high-temperature, and complex environments, ensuring optimal technical solutions for every scenario.

---

### Q: Between pulse solenoid valves and continuous-power solenoid valves, which has longer service life? | comparison,tap

A: Pulse Solenoid Valve service life is far superior to continuous-power Solenoid Valves. Pulse Solenoid Valve: only conducts electricity at the moment of switching (pulse current 10-50ms), does not consume power normally, generates extremely low heat, theoretical lifespan can reach 1,000,000+ cycles. Continuous-power Solenoid Valve: maintains energized state to hold valve position, coil continuously generates heat, long-term operation easily ages and burns out, lifespan typically only 100,000-300,000 cycles. GIBO's entire sensor product line uses self-developed Pulse Solenoid Valves. Tested repair rate is below 5‰. Compared to continuous-power Solenoid Valves, Pulse Solenoid Valves reduce power consumption by 90%+ and extend service life 3-5 times, representing a core advantage configuration for commercial sensor faucets.

---

### Q: Between instant-heating smart toilets and tank-type smart toilets, which is better? | comparison,toilet

A: Main differences between instant-heating and tank-type: Instant-heating uses ceramic instantaneous heating technology (1600W power), live water instant heating, 1-second fast heating. No storage tank means more hygienic water quality, less likely to breed bacteria. Continuous hot water supply with no limit, suitable for multiple people using consecutively. Tank-type has a built-in small water tank (300-500mL) for preheating stored water. Power consumption is lower but water output duration is short (approximately 30-60 seconds before needing to wait for reheating). Long-term water storage in the tank has hygiene concerns. GIBO full-function smart toilets all use instant-heating solutions. Ceramic heating element insulation withstands 3750V/s, service life >10,000 hours. With sufficient budget, choose instant-heating for better experience and hygiene. With limited budget, tank-type can also meet basic needs.

---

### Q: Between sensor soap dispensers and electric push-type soap dispensers, what are the advantages? | comparison,soap

A: Core differences between sensor soap dispensers and electric push-type soap dispensers: Sensor soap dispensers trigger via infrared sensing. Liquid dispenses upon hand reach without touching the machine body, avoiding cross-infection. Suitable for hospitals, food workshops, and other venues with high hygiene level requirements. Electric push-type soap dispensers require hand or arm pressing on the outlet. After multiple users, the outlet easily accumulates dirt and breeds bacteria. Although sensor soap dispensers have a slightly higher unit price (approximately RMB 30-50 more expensive), precise liquid output and reduced waste (adjustable metered output) result in lower comprehensive usage cost. GIBO sensor soap dispensers use independent sensing solutions with high sensitivity and smooth, non-sticking liquid output. Widely used in five-star hotels, office buildings, and medical venues.

---

### Q: Between hot air type and high-speed jet type hand dryers, which has better drying results? | comparison,hand-dryer

A: Hot air type and high-speed jet type each have their strengths. Hot air type: air heated by heating wire before blowing. Output air temperature 40-50℃. Relies on high temperature to evaporate moisture. Drying time approximately 30-40 seconds. Relatively low noise (45-55dB). Moderate energy consumption. High-speed jet type: uses high-speed motor (speed ≥80,000rpm) to generate strong airflow that "scrapes" off moisture. Fast drying (10-15 seconds) but higher noise (75-85dB) and higher energy consumption. Purchasing advice: For star-rated hotels, high-end offices, and other noise-sensitive environments, choose hot air type. For airports, shopping malls, and other high-traffic venues requiring fast hand drying efficiency, choose high-speed jet type. GIBO covers both types and can customize as needed.

---

### Q: Between concealed shower systems and exposed shower systems, which is more suitable for hotels? | comparison,shower

A: Concealed shower: valve body embedded in the wall, only the panel is exposed. Clean and premium appearance. Does not occupy bathroom space. Disadvantage: requires pre-buried piping and concealed boxes before renovation. Complex construction. Later maintenance requires opening the wall. Exposed shower: valve body directly mounted on the wall surface. Simple installation. No need for pre-burying. Convenient later maintenance. Lower cost. Disadvantage: exposed valve body affects aesthetics. Hotel advice: High-star hotels (4-star and above) guest rooms prefer concealed installation for elegance and enhanced room quality. Chain budget hotels and renovation projects choose exposed installation for fast construction and convenient maintenance. GIBO supports both solutions and provides ODM panel appearance customization.

---

### Q: AC power (utility) vs battery-powered sensor faucets. What are the pros and cons of each? | comparison,tap

A: Both have applicable scenarios. AC power: Advantages: no need to replace batteries, suitable for high-frequency use (e.g., shopping malls, airports with 500+ uses/day). Power consumption unrestricted, can carry more functions (digital display, temperature control, etc.). Disadvantages: requires pre-buried power cable. Installation constrained. Stops water during power outages. Battery power: Advantages: flexible installation, no wiring needed. Suitable for renovation projects and venues without power sources. GIBO full series standby current ≤30μA. 4 AA batteries last 4-12 months. Disadvantages: requires periodic battery replacement (daily use below 200 person-times, approximately once every half year). Best solution: GIBO dual power supply (BA/UA system) automatic switching. Uses utility power when available, automatically switches to battery during power outages, balancing installation flexibility and usage reliability.

# Chapter 12 User Experience & Concerns (Tag: user, 10 Entries)

### Q: Are sensor faucets prone to breaking? Is the failure rate higher than ordinary mechanical faucets? | user,tap

A: This is a core concern for many purchasers. In reality, the failure rate of properly branded sensor faucets is not higher than mechanical faucets. GIBO sensor faucet repair rate is below 5‰. Main failure points (solenoid valves, sensor modules) are all modular designs, easy to replace. Common mechanical faucet issues are valve core wear and leakage (after 500,000 cycles), handle loosening, etc. Sensor faucets have additional electronic components, but GIBO's Pulse Solenoid Valves have a service life exceeding 1,000,000 cycles (2x that of mechanical valve cores). Self-developed sensor motherboards undergo high-low temperature aging tests (-20℃~85℃) with extremely low failure rates. Purchasing advice: Choose brands with self-developed technology accumulation (e.g., GIBO with 20 years of self-developed experience). Avoid low-price generic products. GIBO sensor faucets have a 2-year full unit warranty and 3-year warranty on core electronic control components.

---

### Q: Will the instant-heating module of a smart toilet fail? Is replacement cost high? | user,toilet

A: The instant-heating module is the core heating component of a smart toilet. Under normal use, service life is very long (>10,000 hours, approximately 8-10 years). GIBO instant-heating modules use ceramic sintered heating elements (sintered at 1600℃), with insulation withstand voltage of 3750V/s, five-layer overheat protection, high safety, and low likelihood of damage. If a fault occurs, GIBO smart toilets use modular design. The instant-heating module can be replaced individually. Parts cost approximately RMB 200-400. No need for whole unit replacement. Free replacement within the warranty period (3 years). Regularly clean the water inlet filter mesh (recommended every 6 months) to effectively prevent scale from clogging the heating element and extend service life.

---

### Q: What to do when a sensor soap dispenser's liquid output becomes inaccurate after long-term use? | user,soap

A: Inaccurate liquid output from a sensor soap dispenser is usually caused by the following reasons. Troubleshoot in order: (1) Soap liquid too viscous (replace with thinner soap liquid or dilute the original). (2) Outlet nozzle clogged (remove the outlet nozzle and soak in warm water for cleaning). (3) Insufficient battery power (replace with new batteries). (4) Liquid level sensor fault (misjudgment when liquid level is low). Daily maintenance advice: Use soap liquid with good flowability (hand soap/body wash diluted to 60%-80% of original concentration). Clean the outlet nozzle monthly. GIBO sensor soap dispensers use independent sensing solutions and large-aperture peristaltic pumps with high soap liquid compatibility. Under normal use, output precision deviation is <10%.

---

### Q: Under what circumstances are sensor faucets prone to false triggering? How does GIBO avoid this? | user,tap

A: Common scenarios for sensor faucet false triggering: (1) Sensor window directly facing sink counter reflective surface (mirror, stainless steel, etc.), where strong reflections cause misjudgment. (2) Installation position close to entrance or walkway, triggered by passing pedestrians. (3) Pets (dogs, cats) moving within the sensor range. (4) Sudden changes in ambient temperature (e.g., bathroom temperature approaching body temperature after summer sun exposure). GIBO has made specialized optimizations for these scenarios: self-developed Reliability Theory infrared sensing technology with built-in anti-interference algorithms effectively filters non-target trigger signals. Precise sensing angle control (downward 45°) avoids side false triggers. Adjustable sensitivity (adapts to different environments). Following GIBO installation guidance, over 98% of false triggering issues can be avoided.

---

### Q: With elderly and children at home, is the learning curve for using sensor faucets high? | user,tap

A: Zero learning curve. The core advantage of sensor faucets is precisely "water flows when hand reaches out, water stops when hand leaves." Completely no learning needed. Elderly users don't need to bend down to twist handles. Children don't need to reach handles. Especially user-friendly for people with mobility limitations. Compared to traditional mechanical faucets that require hand-contact operation, sensor faucets achieve fully contactless operation, more hygienic and safer. Actual operation experience: Extend hand to faucet sensor area (approximately 10-20cm) → Automatic water output → Wash hands → Hand leaves → Automatic water stop. Simple three steps. Users of any age can master it instantly. GIBO sensor faucets have sensor indicator lights with at-a-glance trigger status.

---

### Q: Can the warm air drying function of a smart toilet really dry completely? How effective is it? | user,toilet

A: The warm air drying function can indeed dry, but requires some time. GIBO smart toilet drying modules use precision brushless fans with wind speeds up to 4m/s, combined with heating wire delivering 40-50℃ warm air. Normal use achieves approximately 80% drying in 1-2 minutes. Complete drying takes approximately 3-5 minutes (depending on personal habits and residual water amount after washing). It is recommended to use water washing first → warm air drying → supplemented with a small amount of tissue to wipe residual moisture, for highest efficiency. In winter, warm air blowing is physically comfortable, more hygienic than using tissue to wipe. Compared to users accustomed to tissue wiping, long-term use of warm air drying can save significant amounts of toilet paper, environmentally friendly.

---

### Q: Does the smart shower's cold water pre-drainage function waste water? | user,shower

A: The Cold Water Pre-drainage function not only does not waste water but actually saves water. Principle: After the user triggers "pre-drainage," the smart shower system automatically opens the lower outlet solenoid valve to drain accumulated cold water from the pipeline. When hot water arrives, it automatically stops (detecting water temperature ≥38℃), then switches to showerhead/overhead shower output. The pre-drained cold water can be collected from the lower outlet nozzle into a bucket (for toilet flushing, floor mopping) or directly drained into the sewer. Compared to "directly turning on the shower to wait for hot water without pre-drainage," the pre-drainage function centrally discharges cold water without splashing everywhere, avoiding the discomfort of being hit by cold water for users while enabling cold water recovery. Default maximum pre-drainage duration is 180 seconds, automatically stops when constant temperature is reached earlier.

---

### Q: Can sensor equipment be used in areas with frequent power outages? | user,tap

A: This depends on the equipment's power supply solution. GIBO provides multiple power supply options: (1) Dual power supply (BA/UA automatic switching): uses utility power when available, automatically switches to battery during power outages. No water stoppage during outages. (2) Pure battery power supply: 4 AA batteries last 4-12 months. Completely unaffected by power outages. (3) AC/DC dual-use hand dryer: can switch to built-in battery for emergency use during power outages. (4) Smart shower: hydropower generator + lithium battery. Self-powered with water flow. Unaffected by power outages. For areas with frequent outages, it is recommended to prioritize dual-power sensor faucets and battery power supply solutions. GIBO full series low-power consumption design (standby ≤30μA). During outages, batteries can support normal use for several weeks to months.

---

### Q: Is the Smart Toilet foam shield feature good? Does the foam liquid get used up quickly? | user,toilet

A: The foam shield feature is one of the most popular add-on features for smart toilets today, with strong practicality. Principle: When the toilet fills with water, it automatically mixes foam liquid to produce a dense foam layer covering the water surface, providing three functions: ① Splash prevention — the foam layer cushions, preventing water from splashing onto the body; ② Odor isolation — the foam layer blocks sewer odors from rising back; ③ Anti-stick — foam adheres to the ceramic surface, making it harder for waste to stick and easier to flush clean. Regarding foam liquid consumption: One standard bottle of foam liquid (approx. 300mL) under normal household use (avg. 5-8 times/day) lasts approximately 30-45 days. Refill packs cost about RMB 20-30/bottle, with a total daily cost under RMB 1. GIBO full-feature Smart Toilets come standard with foam shield and support foam liquid level reminders.

---

# Chapter 13 Installation & Compatibility (Tag: installation, 8 Total)

### Q: What size countertop hole is needed to install a sensor faucet? Can standard basins all accommodate it? | installation,tap

A: GIBO Sensor Faucets primarily use a standard single-hole design (hole diameter 35mm), compatible with over 95% of standard basins on the market (wash basins, kitchen sinks, etc.). Some kitchen sensor faucets use a two-hole or three-hole design with a decorative cover plate to conceal extra holes. Installation key points: Countertop thickness ≤45mm allows direct installation; exceeding 45mm requires extended fixing screws. Vessel sinks, under-mount sinks, and flush-mount sinks are all compatible. If the basin has no pre-drilled hole, a specialized countertop drill bit can be purchased to create a hole (ceramic, quartz, and stainless steel countertops can all be drilled). It is recommended to confirm the basin hole dimensions before purchasing; GIBO provides pre-sales installation compatibility consultation to avoid buying the wrong model.

---

### Q: When upgrading an old toilet to a smart toilet seat, are sizes universal? What should I look out for? | installation,toilet

A: Upgrading an old toilet to a smart toilet seat is not 100% universal. Four key dimensions must be confirmed before purchase: ① Installation hole spacing (the distance between toilet mounting screw holes, commonly 145-185mm); ② Toilet length (from mounting holes to the front edge of the toilet, must be ≥480mm); ③ Toilet shape (V-shaped/U-shaped/D-shaped, which determines whether the seat shape matches); ④ Spacing between the toilet and the wall (room needed for the seat unit). GIBO Smart Toilet Seats are compatible with over 90% of mainstream toilets on the market (Arrow, Faenza, Hegii, etc.), but compatibility with European-style toilets and irregularly shaped toilets is lower. It is recommended to measure according to the "Toilet Compatibility Guide" on the GIBO website or take a photo to contact customer service for confirmation before purchasing. Upgrading a smart toilet seat generally requires no wall or plumbing modifications; DIY installation can be completed in 30 minutes.

### Q: What is the concealed Smart Shower valve body pre-burial size? What needs to be prepared before renovation? | installation,shower

A: GIBO concealed Smart Shower valve body pre-burial dimensions (standard model): The pre-burial box dimensions are generally length 270mm × width 120mm × depth 80mm (tolerance ≤5mm). Before renovation, prepare the following in advance: ① Pre-burial groove — wall groove depth ≥100mm, width ≥300mm, height ≥150mm, for convenient installation of the pre-burial box; ② Hot and cold water pipes — pre-bury two G1/2 female threaded elbows, spacing 150mm (national standard), left hot, right cold; ③ Power cable — if mains power is needed, reserve one 2.5mm² three-core power cable to the pre-burial box location; ④ Signal cable — if connecting a remote control receiver, reserve a signal cable conduit. A: Most GIBO sensor devices have moderate installation difficulty; users with basic hands-on skills and tools can install them themselves. Specific categories: ① Sensor Faucets — Moderate, need to connect water pipes and install batteries (battery model) / connect power (mains model); non-professionals are recommended to hire a plumber (installation fee approx. RMB 50-100/unit); ② Sensor Flush Valves — Easy, wall-mounted only requires two holes for hanging, one-piece version replaces the existing flush valve; ③ Hand Dryers — Moderate, need 220V power connection, electrician installation recommended; ④ Soap Dispensers — Easy, wall-mount or countertop, completed in 10 minutes; ⑤ Smart Showers — Complex (concealed requires pre-burial) / Easy (exposed mount is plug-and-play); ⑥ Smart Toilet Seats — Easy, replace old with new in 30 minutes. All products include detailed installation instructions and QR-code installation videos.

---

### Q: Are Sensor Flush Valves compatible with all toilets? | installation,flusher

A: GIBO Sensor Flush Valves are compatible with over 90% of standard toilets on the market, but the following points must be confirmed:
① One-piece Sensor Flush Valves (replacing original push-button flush valves): The outlet diameter and inlet pipe connection standard (G1/2 or G3/8) must match; cannot adapt to niche brands with special connections.
② Split-type Sensor Flush Valves (wall-mounted + external water pipe): Flexible installation, strong versatility, compatible with most toilets.
③ Concealed Sensor Flush Valves (embedded installation): Requires pre-burial, suitable for new construction projects.
④ Sensor Urinal Flush Valves: Compatible with standard urinals (both wall-hung and floor-standing), nozzle length can be trimmed as needed.
It is recommended to provide the toilet brand/model or actual photo before purchasing; GIBO customer service can help confirm compatible models to ensure proper fit and installation.

**Conclusion**: dTOF Laser is the **most versatile and stable** solution, especially suitable for complex installation environments.

### Q: What is "adaptive ambient light compensation algorithm"? | sensor,company
A: GIBO's self-developed algorithm that automatically adjusts the sensing threshold based on ambient light changes.

**Problems Solved**:
- Dawn/dusk changes: No inconsistent morning/evening detection
- Cloudy/sunny changes: Consistent sensing distance regardless of weather
- Light occlusion: Avoids interference from curtains or shadows

**Effect**: No manual adjustment needed after installation; sensing performance remains consistently stable.

---

### Q: What is "dynamic threshold filtering algorithm"? | sensor,company
A: An intelligent algorithm that filters out brief interference signals.

**Interference It Can Filter**:
- Insects flying past the sensor window
- Water flow reflection flickering
- Curtains fluttering
- Mirror reflection fluctuations

**Effect**: Effectively reduces false-trigger rate; tested false-trigger rate <0.5%.

### Q: What is "staged sleep mode strategy"? | tap,sensor,company

|---------|---------|---------|
| High-Frequency (>100x/day) | Continuous detection | Slightly higher |
| Medium-Frequency (20-100x/day) | 0.5 seconds | 18μA |
| Low-Frequency (<20x/day) | 1 second | 12μA |
| Prolonged Inactivity | Deep sleep | 8μA |

**Effect**: Balances response speed and battery life.

**Recommendation**: Replace batteries within 1 week after seeing the low battery alert.

A: ✅ **Yes. GIBO HW05 thermostatic valve core is sold separately and compatible with standard pre-burial boxes.** But it's recommended to purchase the complete set (valve core + panel + shower head) for unified style and best compatibility.

### Q: How to use the Cold Water Pre-drainage function? Do I need to manually activate it every time? | shower
A: ✅ **Press the pre-drainage button once.** The system automatically detects water temperature and stops at ≥38°C. Press once before your first shower of the day; subsequent use will auto-maintain thermostatic temperature.

### Q: Can the Smart Shower save temperature presets for several people? | shower
A: ✅ **Yes. GIBO supports 3 preset temperature memories (M1/M2/M3), switchable with one touch.** Example: Dad 40°C, Mom 42°C, Baby 38°C.

### Q: Sensor Faucet won't shut off — 5-minute self-repair guide. | tap,trouble
A: ✅ **Four-step self-repair: 1. Replace battery (most common cause) 2. Wipe sensor window (dirt blocking) 3. Clean inlet water filter (abnormal water pressure) 4. Disassemble and clean Solenoid Valve (debris jamming).** 90% of issues are resolved by step 2.

### Q: Sensor Flush Valve makes a persistent hissing sound after flushing? | flusher
A: ✅ **Three causes: Water pressure too high (add pressure reducing valve) + Worn seal gasket (replace gasket) + Air in pipe (bleed air).** The hissing sound won't affect function but should be checked — long-term may damage the valve body.

### Q: Sensor fixtures won't dispense water in winter — is it frozen? | trouble
A: ✅ **Check in order: Battery low (low temperature reduces battery capacity) + Sensor window frosted (wipe it) + Pipe frozen (pour warm water on pipe to thaw).** Indoor standard installation will not freeze.

### Q: Smart Toilet remote doesn't respond but the seat is warm? | toilet,trouble
A: ✅ **Remote battery is dead (most common, replace CR2032 button cell).** Use the emergency button on the side of the toilet to operate temporarily; replace the battery to restore function.

### Q: Sensor Hand Dryer airflow has weakened — what to do? | hand-dryer,trouble
A: ✅ **Clean the air inlet filter (dust clogging is most common) + Check the motor (3+ years of use may need maintenance).** Clean the filter monthly to maintain airflow.

### Q: Changed to new batteries but the Sensor Faucet still won't dispense water? | tap,trouble
A: ✅ **Troubleshooting order: Battery installed backwards (check polarity) + Battery box poor contact (wipe contacts) + Water supply valve closed (check angle valve) + Filter completely clogged (disassemble and clean).**

### Q: Worried about not getting used to a Sensor Faucet — any way to try it out first? | tap,user
A: ✅ **GIBO offers 7-day no-reason return (uninstalled/unused).** Try a Sensor Faucet at a hotel (many high-end hotels have widely adopted them), or purchase a MINI Sensor Nozzle (under RMB 300) for a low-cost trial.

### Q: Biggest concern when buying sensor sanitary ware is no after-sales support — how does GIBO guarantee this? | company,user
A: ✅ **Triple protection: 3-year warranty + 7 national service centers + spare parts warehouse inventory.** After-sales hotline: 0591-88066000, online response within 30 minutes. Fuzhou-based enterprise — won't disappear or fold.

### Q: Worried about Smart Toilet electrical leakage — is it safe? | toilet,trouble,user
A: ✅ **Five-layer safety protection: Leakage protection switch + Overheat protection + IPX4 waterproofing + 3,750V insulation withstand voltage + Flame-retardant housing material.** GIBO Smart Toilets pass CCC/CE safety certification — safer than electric water heaters.

### Q: Sensor Soap Dispenser dispenses inaccurate amounts — how to calibrate? | soap
A: ✅ **Cannot be manually calibrated; switch between 1-3 levels via remote control to adjust dispensing volume.** If deviation exceeds ±30%, contact after-sales for pump replacement (free within warranty).

### Q: What is the standard installation height for a Hand Dryer? | hand-dryer,installation
A: ✅ **Standard adult: 1200-1300mm (bottom from floor). Children: 900-1000mm. Accessible: 800-900mm.** Measure with a ruler before installation; secure with expansion bolts.

### Q: Do I need extra accessories to install a Sensor Faucet on a two-hole basin countertop? | tap
A: ✅ **A decorative cover plate is needed (to cover extra holes).** GIBO includes the cover plate as standard — no additional purchase required. For non-standard hole spacing, contact customer service to confirm the solution.

### Q: Not enough space under the counter for the Sensor Faucet control box — what to do? | installation,tap,trouble
A: ✅ **Choose wall-mount installation (external bracket) or side concealed installation (cabinet side panel).** Control box dimensions approx. 120×80×60mm; measure the under-counter space in advance.

### Q: How often should the Smart Toilet inlet water filter be cleaned? | toilet
A: ✅ **Recommended every 6 months; every 3 months in hard water areas (northern China).** Cleaning method: Close inlet angle valve → Unscrew filter → Rinse with clean water → Reinstall. A completely clogged filter will cause reduced water output.

### Q: GIBO vs Kohler Sensor Faucet — technology route differences? | company,comparison,tap
A: ✅ **GIBO: Self-developed dTOF Laser (independent of reflection). Kohler: Outsourced infrared (dependent on reflection).** dTOF advantages: Stable with black objects / mirrored surfaces / strong light. Kohler advantages: Brand recognition + design.

### Q: How are domestic Sensor Faucet brands tiered (Tier 1, 2, 3)? | company,tap
A: ✅ **Tier 1 (GIBO / professional sensor focus 20 years + national standard drafter). Tier 2 (Jomoo / Arrow / comprehensive sanitary ware, sensor as secondary line). Tier 3 (White-label / Taobao unbranded / no R&D, no certification).** Commercial engineering projects must choose Tier 1.

### Q: Pulse Solenoid Valve vs Motor Valve — which is better for commercial high-frequency use? | comparison,sensor
A: ✅ **Pulse Solenoid Valve wins hands down.** Pulse type: instant power only (low power + 1 million cycle lifespan). Motor valve: continuous rotation needed (high power + 200K-300K cycle lifespan). Commercial scenarios with 200+ daily uses → pulse type lasts 3-5 years without replacement.

### Q: Integrated Smart Toilet vs Toilet + Smart Seat — how much lifespan difference? | comparison,toilet
A: ✅ **Integrated unit design life: 8-10 years. Seat: 5-8 years.** Integrated unit has larger internal space and better heat dissipation, extending electronic component lifespan. The seat is limited by space and dissipates heat less effectively.

### Q: AC power vs Battery vs Hydro-generator — full lifecycle cost comparison? | comparison,flusher,trouble
A: ✅ **Hydro-generator (lowest 10-year total cost / zero maintenance) + Battery (lowest initial cost / replace once yearly ~RMB 10) + AC (unlimited runtime / requires pre-burial / monthly electricity ~RMB 2).** Choose based on the specific scenario.

### Q: Cold-only vs Hot/Cold dual-inlet Sensor Faucet — which to choose? | comparison,shower,tap
A: ✅ **Have a water heater and don't want cold water handwashing in winter → choose dual-inlet. No water heater or warm southern climate → cold-only is sufficient and saves cost.** Dual-inlet requires connecting both hot and cold pipes, slightly more complex installation.

### Q: What international certifications do GIBO products hold? Which export markets do they cover? | company
A: ✅ **CE (EU-wide) + RoHS (EU environmental) + FCC (US wireless) + CUPC (US/Canada plumbing) + NSF (US drinking water) + WaterMark (Australia) + WRAS (UK) + ACS (France).** Full coverage — no barriers to export.

### Q: Why are GIBO prices so much lower than foreign brands? | company
A: ✅ **Factory direct — no distributor markup + Self-developed technology saves external licensing fees + China manufacturing supply chain advantage.** Foreign brands carry a 100-300% brand premium for the same specs.

### Q: Are Sensor Faucets a "gimmick tax"? | tap

Everyone who's used one says it's worth it. The only "tax" is buying a no-name brand — poor quality, dead within a year.

A: ✅ **Full product line complies with RoHS hazardous substance restrictions + ISO14001 environmental management system + recyclable packaging.** Products export to EU/North America without barriers.

1. **Volume-price linkage**: 50 units → standard engineering price, 200 units → 10% off, 500 units → 20% off, 1,000+ → exclusive quotation
2. **Bundle negotiation**: Purchasing Sensor Faucet + Flush Valve + Soap Dispenser together can get 5-10% more discount than single-category orders

### Q: How many standard Sensor Faucet mounting hole distances are there? Can old-style two-hole basins accommodate them? | installation,tap

A: ✅ **Standard single hole 35mm. Two-hole and three-hole basins can also be accommodated.**

| Basin Type | Installation Solution |
|---------|---------|
| Single Hole (35mm) | ✅ Direct installation |
| Two-Hole (spacing 102mm / 4 inches) | ✅ Use decorative cover plate to conceal extra holes |
| Three-Hole (spacing 203mm / 8 inches) | ✅ Use larger decorative cover plate |
| Non-Standard Hole | Contact customer service to confirm compatibility solution |

---

### Q: Precautions for drilling holes in marble / quartz countertops? | installation

A: ✅ **Stone countertop drilling requires professional tools; hiring a stone specialist is recommended.**

| Note | Description |
|--------|------|
| Drill Bit | Use diamond drill bit (φ35mm) |
| Cooling | Drill while watering to cool and prevent stone cracking |
| Position | ≥5cm from basin edge, leave room for faucet rotation |
| Thickness | Countertop ≤45mm supports standard installation; >45mm requires extension accessories |

💡 It is recommended to have the stone specialist review the installation drawing and confirm the solution before purchasing.

---

| Side Concealed | Install inside the basin side cabinet or behind a decorative panel |
| External Waterproof Box | Add IP65 waterproof enclosure, installed exposed |

### Q: Can I install an AC-powered Sensor Faucet in an old house with aging electrical wiring? | tap

A: ✅ **Recommend choosing the battery model or dual-power model.**

Aging electrical wiring has two risks: ① Voltage instability may damage the controller; ② No ground wire poses a safety hazard. Battery models completely avoid these issues — 4x AA batteries last 1.5-2 years.
